# Patch 1.18.1 — Nightmares of Ursol

## Post 154168 by Torta (Turtle WoW Team) [STAFF] — 2025-10-03T10:13:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154168#p154168 | page 1 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/NoU_04_empty.jpg)

Hello, everyone! We're excited to announce that we're beginning to publish preliminary patch notes for our upcoming update, Patch 1.18.1 — **Nightmares of Ursol**. This post will be updated regularly as new information becomes available before the patch releases.

The new patch will bring **Moonwhisper Coast**, a brand-new late leveling zone, **Windhorn Canyon**, one challenging dungeon, **Timbermaw Hold**, an all-new 20 man raid designed to be harder than the existing 20 man raids. In addition, the iconic Vanilla raids **Onyxia Lair** and **Blackwing Lair** will receive some fresh touches and improvements. There'll be new PvP events, Survival profession rework, new items and much-much more! Stay tuned!

[embed: https://www.youtube.com/embed/-O2lcZGUQZs]

 **NEW ZONE — MOONWHISPER COAST**
 *Zone Level: 50-56*

Clad in moonlit splendor, this stretch of Kaldorei land is as mysterious as it is bewitched. Spirits of forlorn Highborne haunt the abandoned elven ruins and alien magic permeates the blue forests of this humid shore. An unbound land only a few would willingly call their home, yet it became such a haven for the wandering Tauren Tribe of the Moonhoof, now holding watch over the azure expanses of the chaotic Moonwhisper Coast.

[![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/build/assets/Moonwhisper_Coast-Be-tD1Vx.jpg)](https://turtlecraft.gg/build/assets/Moonwhisper_Coast-Be-tD1Vx.jpg)

 **NEW DUNGEON — WINDHORN CANYON**
 *New 5-Man Dungeon [26-30]*

This ancient canyon has been home to many tauren tribes who have in years past fought for the dominance of its flowing waters and shelter from the dangers of Kalimdor.

The cultures and traditions of many have lived within Windhorn Canyon, which can be seen from the ancient shelters carved into the mountainside, to the relics coveted by the Tauren.

As of recently the Windhorn Tauren were expelled and driven away by the Grimtotem who have conquered it, and claimed it for their own.

 **NEW RAID — TIMBERMAW HOLD**
 *New 20-Man Raid*

As ancient as Kalimdor itself, this enigmatic labyrinthine network of tunnels and caves beneath Mount Hyjal has been the home of the Furbolgs since well before the Sundering. Its halls are sacred among the tribes, a place of worship to their progenitors, the twin gods Ursoc and Ursol. Nowadays however, only wafts of putrid vapors escape the rotten caverns and whispers of venerating a foul god echo throughout Timbermaw Hold…

 **ONYXIA'S LAIR EXTENSION & UPDATES**

The broodmother of the Black Dragonflight has fought back many incursions into her lair, each inching closer to her demise. For this reason the security in her lair has been elevated, now featuring new guardians, the commander of her brood as a brand new boss and new tricks for Onyxia herself, the nest is now more perilous than ever. Lucky for you, peril attracts treasure, adding plenty of new equipment as rewards.

- Added two new creatures to Onyxia's Lair: **Onyxian Flamespawn** and **Onyxian Inciter**.

- Added a new section and with it a new optional boss: **Broodcommander Axelus**.

- Added new mechanics to Onyxia.
[![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/ony_3.png)](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/ony_3.png)

| [![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/ony_2.png)](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/ony_2.png) | [![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/ony_1.png)](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/ony_1.png) | [![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/ony_4.png)](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/ony_4.png) |

 **BLACKWING LAIR EXTENSION & UPDATES**

Lord Victor Nefarius recently realized that his pitiful minions have been insufficient in preventing interlopers from walking up to his doorstep. Therefore he has taken steps in improving his entourage, with no small amount of help from his newly contracted alchemist.

Ezzel Darkbrewer might be a goblin of small stature but he is by no means a pushover. With an arsenal of chemical concoctions and a brilliant mind sharpened by many years in the field, he's ready to thwart any intruders stepping into his employer's laboratory. Oh, and don't forget the lumbering ogre he's atop of.

- Added a new mechanic or updated a mechanic for all bosses (with the exception of Chromaggus).

- Added a new section and with it a new optional boss: **Ezzel Darkbrewer**.
[![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/bwl_addition.jpg)](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/bwl_addition.jpg)

 **CLASSIC ZONE EDITS**

- **Sagh's Refuge**. A new quest hub in **Thousand Needles** for **Horde** players located in **Windhorn Canyon**.

- **Ironstone Camp.**Updated **Ironstone Camp**in **Thousand Needles** with more details.

- **Earthern Ring.** Earthen Ring has been restored as a sanctuary for shamans to train and work towards restoring balance to Azeroth.

- **Azshara** has been updated to show the **Timbermaw Hold Gate** and the furbolgs that live along the cliffs above it. The Timbermaw Hold Gate allows transport to the new raid, as well as between **Moonwhisper Coast** and **Azshara**.

 **NEW FACTIONS**

- **Draenei Exiles [Neutral].** The Draenei Tribes from Outland that have survived the rigors of warp travel and now find themselves upon new lands. The **Sanv** and the **Moro'gai** tribes now reside on Azeroth, brought to the planet by adept riftwalkers. The Draenei Exiles now seek to make a new chapter of prosperity for their people.

- **Earthen Ring [Horde].** A group of shamans, united in their quest for elemental balance, has made their home in the serene Stonetalon Mountains.

 **NEW RACE & CLASS COMBINATION — TAUREN PRIEST**

Guided by the gaze of the celestial siblings, the children of the Earthmother have faced many hardships while still staying true to their deities. Their efforts to rekindle the ancestral bond between the celestial and the mortal have granted them the boon of the Three Siblings. Yet while Mu'sha and An'she seek to bless those of good will, the younger sibling Lo'sho is known to answer any who seek his blessing, regardless of intent. And so the fate of the Horde twists under the gaze of the Earthmother, as Tauren across the lands seek to represent and spread their cultural worship, either through acts of hope and order or dismay and chaos.

[![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/taurenpriest.png)](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/taurenpriest.png)

 **NEW RACE & CLASS COMBINATION — DWARF WARLOCK**

Power is alluring, even more so if it is forbidden. The noble Bronzebeard dwarves are no exception to that. In the wake of the gnomes' refuge in Ironforge, some of their more morally flexible members have established a secret coven within the mountain city, practicing the shadowy arts of demonology away from the gaze of the simple folk. Initially cautious, they have accepted many eager dwarves into their ranks, teaching them the heretical summoning of demons and subservience to their dark masters…

[![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/dwarfwarlock.png)](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/dwarfwarlock.png)

 **PVP DAMPENING**

The PvP Dampening Debuff introduced in 1.18.0 will now have dynamic reductions of Magical and Physical damage based on your class / spec instead of a flat number for all classes.

 **NEW BATTLEGROUND — THORN GORGE**

Introducing a **15v15** Eye of the Storm-style battleground.

Teams capture flags and bases until they reach the maximum resource points needed to win.

[![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/amberhorn_bg.png)](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/amberhorn_bg.png)

 **Sunnyglade Valley — Under Construction!** Sunnyglade Valley, our first dedicated PvP map from 2021, didn't turn out to be a pinnacle of game design. Therefore, Patch 1.18.1 disables the battleground, and in future patches we will focus on a full rework to make it far more engaging and balanced.

 **WORLD PVP EVENTS — REDWALL KEEP, SOUTHSHORE VS TARREN MILL**

Take part in the upcoming open‑world PvP events: conquer **Redwall Keep**, defend **Tarren Mill** or **Southshore**!

Honor your faction by joining these battles and gain exclusive rewards!

 **Learn more about PvP Changes in 1.181.1: [viewtopic.php?t=24132](https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?t=24132)**

 **SURVIVAL SKILL OVERHAUL**

As our first profession, **Survival Skill** was held back by limited resources and inexperience. The ideas were fresh and exciting, and while many concepts, such as tents and gardening, received great reception after refinements, the profession ultimately did not live up to its potential.

Survival was originally scrapped during the vanilla development cycle. Because of this, we had a blank slate to work with and could steer the profession toward a role that fits alongside the others. For that reason, we decided to give Survival a more complementary function with an extensive overhaul.

The new, reworked Survival profession will act as a secondary profession that draws from other primary professions and supplements them with advantages for players who raise their Survival skill further. This is reflected in the over 80 new recipes added to the profession. For example, skilled Survivalists can create powerful tools that can slow, root, or even trap opponents. They can also craft bags suitable for the three gathering professions, as well as containers for fish and meat. This provides a unique experience in creating and enhancing synergy between different professions.

To give Survival additional depth, we are linking it to a new gathering activity: **Woodcutting**. Directly tied to your Survival skill, Woodcutting lets you search forests in the open world for wood and other materials needed for the profession.

Of course, **Gardening** will also receive several new additions. We will introduce three new crops that players can find in specific zones throughout the world. When fully grown, these crops will grant powerful buffs to ambitious gardeners. Additionally, reports indicate that unwelcome critters have begun infiltrating the crops of unsuspecting adventurers…

With this overhaul, we aim to cement Survival's place among the other professions and elevate it to a one‑of‑a‑kind position not seen before in Warcraft. We hope you'll have plenty of fun scouring the world for materials and using the many tools to increase your chances of surviving the wilds!

 **Tents & Taverns**

- Both taverns and tents now grant the rested XP bonus at the same pace.

- Rested XP from killing creatures was reduced to a 50% bonus instead of the previous 100% bonus.

- Tent's rested XP bonus was capped at 25% of a player's rested XP, tents provide no benefit if the player has more than 25% rested XP.

- All taverns except those located in cities can now raise a player's rested XP up to 100%.

[![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/survival.png)](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/survival.png)

 **NEW CHALLENGE — TRIAL OF HEROISM**
 *Levelling Challenge*

Earn the **Hero of Azeroth** title by walking the hard road! Players undergoing this challenge will only earn XP from orange/red‑difficulty sources, both creatures or quests. The challenge ends at level 58.

[![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/trial_of_heroism.jpg)](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/trial_of_heroism.jpg)

 **CLASS CHANGES**

Please note that this is a preliminary changelog. Further changes may still be made: [viewtopic.php?p=166071#p166071](https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=166071#p166071)

 **ITEMIZATION CHANGELOG**

We're happy to present to you the itemization changelog for this patch. As always, keep in mind all changes are subject to change: [viewtopic.php?f=70&t=23973](https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?f=70&t=23973)

 **GENERAL CHANGES**

- **Shieldrender Talisman** can now be triggered by ranged physical attacks.

- Fixed POI icons alignment and prevented spill from other icons.

- Resolved UI error “attempt to index local fadeInfo (a nil value)" that occurred when hovering over chat.

- **Arenas** are back on the minimap battleground finder.

- **Character Sheet** now correctly displays the damage multiplier.

- Fixed missing creature pack linking in the **Tower of Karazhan**.

- Removed a decoration from the **Caverns of Time** that often caused players to get stuck.

- Removed collision from the **Remote Mail Terminal**, **Goblin Brainwashing Device** and **Small Winter Veil Tree**.

- Added collision to **Hot Coals** in the **Molten Core** Majordomo Executus encounter.

- Added a [shortcut](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/bwl_door.png) to skip the **Suppression Room** after defeating **Broodlord Lashlayer**.

- Updated **Raka'shishi**'s proc effect name to “Adrift Strikes".

- Clarified the **Restoration Shaman** Tier 1 3‑set bonus description: it now increases totem range by +10 yards (instead of setting it to 30 yards).

- Added missing vanilla rare spawn **Baron Bloodbane**.

- Re‑added the **Alchemy Lab** to the **Tower of Karazhan**.

- Fixed multiple issues with the Shaman's **Earthquake** talent.

- **Hourglass Sand** now also removes the pet debuff **Brood Affliction: Bronze** from your active pet.

- Improved **Stack Split Frame**: you can now adjust split amounts with the mouse wheel, use Shift + mouse wheel to snap to min/max, and Shift + click to purchase vendor items in stacks greater than 1.

- Increased the number of pushable UI panels that can be opened simultaneously.

- Raid assistants can now change raid member groups (previously only the raid leader could).

- Chosen radio station is now saved and will resume playing after relog/reload.

- **Lower Karazhan Halls** now appear as a separate instance instead of being grouped under the Tower of Karazhan on the social tab or map.

- Fixed a ground hole in the **Badlands**.

- Fixed a water pool that should be a lava pool in the **Searing Gorge**.

- Corrected the **The Highlander's Intent** set bonus: the 1% spell‑critical‑chance bonus now requires a 3‑piece set instead of 2 pieces.

- Adjusted cooking recipes for more variety:
 • **Hot Smoked Bass**: 10 Spirit → 30 Spirit.
 • **Cooked Glossy Mightfish**: 10 Stamina → 15 Spell Penetration.
 • **Mightfish Steak**: 10 Stamina → 20 Strength.
 • **Dirge's Kickin' Chimaerok Chops**: 25 Stamina → 30 Stamina + 2% Dodge.

- Added two new cooking recipes:
 • **Squid Eel Skewer** — grants 20 Agility.
 • **Deep Sea Stew** — grants 20 health / 5s and 150 Armor.

- Increased the duration of the **Aquadynamic Fish Attractor** to 10 minutes.

- Optimized performance of **Stormwrought Castle** and **Dragonmaw Retreat**; eliminated possible memory leaks and crashes and polished the entrance to Dragonmaw Retreat.

- Added new crafted ammunition for guns in Engineering: **Enchanted Thorium Shells**.

- Added basic search functionality to the guild frame.

- **Enchanted Thorium** now requires 3 [x]Illusion Dust[/x] instead of Dream Dust for crafting.

- Fixed the **Mirror Magic** (Mage T3.5 3‑piece) set bonus so it now triggers correctly: initial hits from Flamestrike, Cone of Cold, Frost Nova, Blast Wave, and Arcane Explosion now have a chance to trigger a rewind effect (Arcane spells deal +10% damage, Frost/Fire spells +20% damage on rewind).

- Added missing hair dyes for **High Elf** players to the hair‑dye vendors.

- Tower of Karazhan — Trash: **Enveloped Flames** will no longer be cast on pets or summons.

- **Astral Insight** reworked: players receive a 5‑second warning before the effect is cast, giving them time to react and stop casting.

- Changed the pathing of the **Greater Gloomwing** next to the instance portal in the Tower of Karazhan.

- Echo of Medivh — **Doom of Medivh** now fades from players when Echo of Medivh is defeated, **Corruption of Medivh** will no longer be cast on the main threat target.

- Chess encounter — **King's Fury** now cleanses all players from Dark Subservience; the **Knight** will no longer occasionally fall through the ground during the encounter.

- Updated all important vanilla and legacy furbolg creatures to use the new armored models.

- Fixed an issue that caused music to not play on the **Isle of Eternal Autumn**.

- **Blossoming Bud** now has a duration of 20 seconds.

- Fixed minimap artifacts in **Timbermaw Tunnels** and the **Gnomeregan Reclamation Facility**.

- **King Morogo Thunderfoot**'s Bash ability can now be prevented by auras that grant stun immunity.

- Fixed an issue with the slaughterhouse in the **Noxious Glade**.

- Fixed broken spell visual for **Sagefish Delight**.

- Removed the **Uldaman Cave** from Bael Modan — any remaining titan activity should be reported to Prospector Khazgorm.

- Added 3.3.5 **Barber Shop** to the game.

- All non‑special **[Satyr](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/3D-Models-008-satyr-woman.jpg)** are now male or female at random.

- Added mailboxes and innkeepers to **Moonglade** and the **Earthen Ring**.

- Fixed the **Warlord's Sanctuary** set using wrong textures for helmet and shoulders.

- Reduced the cooldown to craft **Fishing Boats** by 1 hour.

- **Keyrings can now hold up to 32 keys.**

- Fixed an issue causing the **Grandstaff of the Shen'dralar Elder** to not proc from helpful spells like Rejuvenation or Renew.

- **Flight Path** added between Ambershire, Northwind and Thorium Point, Searing Gorge.

- Updated required materials for **Empowering Herbal Salad**: Sungrass  →  Lush Cabbage Head, Sweet Mountain Berry  →  Moonwhisper Berry.

- Fixed an issue where wands did not benefit from school‑specific damage modifiers correctly.

- Fixed an issue causing the Priest talent **Darkness** to not increase shadow damage of abilities correctly.

- Quests that require payment upon completion now display a pop‑up warning when turned in.

- Back‑end updates to the **Show Player Challenges** option: cache size reductions, automatic cache wipe on major patch releases, and more responsive tooltips.

- Added a new **Undress** button to the shop preview model.

- Visual updates to the **2FA Frame** displayed at the login screen.

- Visual updates to the **Delete Character** and **Rename Character** pop‑up dialogs on the character selection screen.

- **Hunter shots** now have a **cast bar**.

- Added a **sellPrice** return value to **GetItemInfo**.

- **CheckIneractDistance** now accepts range index ‘5’ for a 40 yd check.

- **GetLootMethod** now additionally returns the raid index of the master looter.

- Added **maxStack** and **itemLink** return values to **GetAuctionSellItemInfo**.

- **EditMacro** and **CreateMacro** now accept file names for icon parameters.

- **Simple Wood Planter** is no longer unique.

- **Kruul**'s "Wings of Oblivion cannot be resisted anymore and will always knock players back.

- Restored the cooldown of The **Scythe of Elune**'s "Elune's Wrath" proc.

- **Rethevus**will now immediately enter combat with the player starting **The Dreamer's Challenge**.

- Players may now undergo **Rethevus**' challenge once again after completing **The Dreamer's Challenge**.

- World Bosses have now become summonable! Players can now find powerful artifacts hidden within raid dungeons. Combined with materials found throughout the world, these artifacts allow the summoning of a specific World Boss depending on the artifact found.

- Improved cross-faction battleground queues to ensure more consistent team balance. We will continue to monitor queue behavior closely and make further adjustments as needed.

- **Lesser Mystic Wand** and **Greater Mystic Wand** now require **Bright Wood** instead of **Star Wood**.

- **Elixir of Greater Agility** no longer stacks with **Concoction of the Emerald Mongoose**.

- **Juju Power** no longer stacks with **Concoction of the Arcane Giant**.

- Fixed several issues with **Juju Power** not stacking with item procs, such as **The Untamed Blade** of **Fist of the Forgotten Order**.

- **Dissolvent and Corrosive Poison** now only affect Elementals, Giants, Undead, and Mechanical enemies.[/y]

- **In-game options** have been redesigned: the three original frames (Video, Sound, Interface) are now merged into a single unified frame, improving overall look and feel.

- The main game menu has been updated, removing obsolete buttons, add‑ons that modify the menu should remain unaffected.

- Default values for several options have changed — for example, **Show Buff Durations** is now enabled by default.

- Added a new **Group Frame** that can replace the existing party and raid frames; it is fully customizable via the in‑game options menu.

- Added a search box to the **Keybindings** frame, which is now also movable.

[![Image](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/1181_options.png)](https://turtlecraft.gg/images/patch_1181/1181_options.png)

I'm really excited for the new content here. However after the last patch, I remain pretty confused about the decision to change vanilla bosses. Couldn't figure out how to do it here, but I think it would be a good idea to run a poll on how many people actually want raids like BWL and MC to become harder and more complicated.

I'm all for optional hard content but it feels as if the game will continue to become less accessible for casual players and guilds. I already had to merge the MC runs I was doing with another guild, as our guild runs (that consist mostly of players new to raiding) were no longer doable in good time. Please just keep in mind that not everyone has been playing for 20 years, and not everyone is overgeared for the tier of content.

I imagine even groups that have BWL on farm will not appreciate the extra time investment (as inevitably, people will want loot from the boss). Optional bosses will still be done in most runs. Correct me if I'm wrong but I feel that the vast majority of players would just appreciate new content, **expand the world and preserve the old stuff**.

## Post 154226 by Vinzer75x — 2025-10-03T14:38:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154226#p154226 | page 1 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Torta wrote: Fri Oct 03, 2025 2:30 pm**
> > **Isvya wrote: Fri Oct 03, 2025 2:27 pm**
> > Nothing about new pvp sets for all specs, rated arena rewards, crossfaction, everything that was promised are forgotten except "new pvp events"?
> >
> >  Summonavle world bosses when? Been promised more than a year ago
>
>   What's so difficult to understand about the patch notes being "to be continued"?

It's not that the community doesn't understand "to be continued." They understand it perfectly.

It means that all the other new, exciting content is ready, but the promised PvP features are not, and there's no clear timeline for when they will be. For a community that already feels neglected and has to deal with broken systems and a lack of incentive, a "to be continued" note is less a promise and more a confirmation that PvP is a lower priority.

There's a big and valuable community waiting for these changes, and their engagement is vital for the long-term health of Turtle WoW.

No, me too and I don't even pvp. It's extremely frustrating to purge something in a dungeon or raid only to see "Resist" and then have no way to do the mechanic for 8 seconds until I can try again. Mobs in the open world instantly recast buffs like lightning shield once when you purge them before the ability enters cooldown, which means that a utility skill which was situationally useful while leveling is now actually a *detriment* because you're spending mana and a GCD to have absolutely no effect as the mob frame-1 recasts their self-buff and your ability to remove it enters cooldown.

I don't care if they nerf it for pvp, but as a pve ability it feels **terrible** right now. I used to take pride in purging mobs of frost armor in dungeons so that my melee party mates didn't have their attack speed nerfed, why does that have to suffer?

## Post 154324 by Xenzuly — 2025-10-04T12:27:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154324#p154324 | page 2 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

Class Balance in PvP
PvP balance on Turtle really needs to be reworked. Pure DPS, CC DPS, and Hybrid DPS are not equal.

Hybrids Too Strong
Retribution Paladins are melee, but they also heal, bubble, and outlast. Hunters can fight toe-to-toe in melee while keeping ranged pressure. This feels wrong for hybrids.

CC + Damage
Rogues already have massive CC, but they also out-damage pure melee in a straight fight. That’s not balanced.

Pure DPS Weakness
Pure DPS classes have fewer tools, but they also don’t dominate in their one role. If you are pure melee, you should be the strongest in melee. If ranged, you should control at range. Hybrids should win through versatility, not by being stronger everywhere.

War Mode Rules
Players with War Mode off should not be able to attack those with it on. They should have to return to a main city to re-enable it before PvP.

Unfair Advantage
Right now, War Mode off means they can wait for the best moment to strike, while those with it on are always vulnerable. This undermines the system and makes it unfair.

Commitment Needed
If players had to fully commit to War Mode, PvP would feel fairer and more consistent for everyone.

## Post 154325 by Xenzuly — 2025-10-04T12:39:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154325#p154325 | page 2 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

Another idea: Cross-faction should be optional and limited under War Mode. If I enable War Mode, the opposite faction should not be able to whisper, trade, or directly communicate with me outside of world chat. It breaks immersion when, after killing someone, they can just whisper insults. Cross-faction grouping for dungeons or raids is fine, but outside of that, there shouldn’t be direct interaction — only combat.

## Post 154420 by Dannyp92 (Barrens Chat Casualty) — 2025-10-05T09:50:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154420#p154420 | page 3 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

Changes need to be made to the "War Mode" Challenge. Right now the system heavily incentivizes BGs over World PvP, despite the name of the challenge. Currently, War Mode feels like a "BG Mode with a splash of inconvenient ganking". The incentive structure needs to match with the philosophy stated for the challenge: To wage war on the opposite faction in the open world. BGs are simply more attractive at the moment. A few ideas or inspiration for tweaks to actually incentivize World PvP (though these are not perfect):

 1.Increase world PvP XP dramatically: Make player kills worth 5-10 normal mobs instead of 2. Currently the risk/reward is terrible.

 2.Add zone control bonuses: If your faction controls certain contested zones, grant +15-20% XP/honor in those zones. This creates objectives worth fighting over.

 3.Reduce or remove the BG XP bonus from War Mode: The +20% BG bonus contradicts the "wage war in the open world" philosophy. War Mode should reward world PvP, not make BGs even better.

 4.Add world PvP objectives: Capture points, supply caravans to attack/defend, assassination targets - things that happen in the world, not instances.

 5.Flip the bonuses: +20% world XP, +5% BG XP for War Mode users. This would actually align with the stated goal for the challenge.

On top of this, Faction Leaders could be targets. Perhaps with rewards for those that defend them as well? The implementation could be challenging, but perhaps there should be a "zone" around the NPC, so if the NPC survives within a certain time limit of being attacked, all defenders within the zone get rewards?

## Post 154719 by Metaalverf (Barrens Chat Casualty) — 2025-10-07T13:06:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154719#p154719 | page 3 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Vinzer75x wrote: Fri Oct 03, 2025 2:38 pm**
> > **Torta wrote: Fri Oct 03, 2025 2:30 pm**
> > What's so difficult to understand about the patch notes being "to be continued"?
>
>  For a community that already feels neglected and has to deal with broken systems and a lack of incentive, a "to be continued" note is less a promise and more a confirmation that PvP is a lower priority.

Too be honest, I don't think this is fair to be expected from a fan-based project. While they might be making a profit by now, we should still be aware that TurtleWoW team has been way smaller than Blizzards. In addition, no monthly playtime we pay, so it is not like they owe us anything. In my opinion, lot's of people in the suggestion section ask for ridiculous stuff and then feels neglected because their idea is not being implemented.

As for PvP, I think getting a 'balanced' PvP system while keeping the vanilla vibe is almost impossible. In addition, the PvP realm has been way less populated, hence, it is not strange to assume that PvE content is being viewed as more valuable and thus prioritized by the developers.

Anyways, thanks TurtleWoW for this update. I'm looking forward to it!

## Post 154728 by Isvya (Patch Note Conspiracy Theorist) — 2025-10-07T13:54:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154728#p154728 | page 3 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Metaalverf wrote: Tue Oct 07, 2025 1:06 pm**
> As for PvP, I think getting a 'balanced' PvP system while keeping the vanilla vibe is almost impossible. In addition, the PvP realm has been way less populated, hence, it is not strange to assume that PvE content is being viewed as more valuable and thus prioritized by the developers.
>
>  Anyways, thanks TurtleWoW for this update. I'm looking forward to it!

There's a reason why the pvp realm is less populated:
There's barely any pvp content added. And the quality of the few is just so poor (see sunnyglade and arena)
You can spend months of getting r14 for what? The same old sets and weapons that you can easily upgrade just by running aq and naxx for a couple of weeks. Not to talk about that there's only one version of the sets for 1 generic spec meanwhile you can pick any spec for your raid tier.
And you get demolished by k40 geared overtuned (bcs those  poor pve specs needed absurd amount of buffs) hybrids.

Thats the reason. Theres nothing that makes the server attractive to pvp players. The only thing might be arena until they find out its utterly bugged and you can use all consumables and long CDs and itemswaps.

On the other hand i dont think either you can balance vanilla pvp to a comfortable level, i'd just like to see the willingness to move in the right direction and looking forward to see what else is gonna be added to the patch notes.

## Post 154734 by Xudo (Grandmaster of Forum PvP) — 2025-10-07T14:56:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154734#p154734 | page 3 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

We don't need better pvp gear. We need weaker pve gear!
Buffing pvp sets to pve level will only encourage burst meta, which is undesirable result.

Ideally, raids should be totally removed from PvP server.
No raids = no need to buff hybrids to specialist level.
No raids = no powercreep.
The only real argument for raids is that they are good place for one raid to corpsecamp another raid on the way to entrance. But vanilla also had worldbosses and no-crossfaction for this.

## Post 154759 by Metaalverf (Barrens Chat Casualty) — 2025-10-07T18:45:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=154759#p154759 | page 3 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Isvya wrote: Tue Oct 07, 2025 1:54 pm**
> You can spend months of getting r14 for what? The same old sets and weapons that you can easily upgrade just by running aq and naxx for a couple of weeks. Not to talk about that there's only one version of the sets for 1 generic spec meanwhile you can pick any spec for your raid tier.
>  And you get demolished by k40 geared overtuned (bcs those  poor pve specs needed absurd amount of buffs) hybrids.
>
>  Thats the reason. Theres nothing that makes the server attractive to pvp players. The only thing might be arena until they find out its utterly bugged and you can use all consumables and long CDs and itemswaps.

Too be honest, I think PvE has always been about progression (of gear) and PvP merely about testing your progression to those of other players. I think PvP gear was only there to provide some sort of reward to show of to others. It had nothing to do with becoming equivalent to PvE gear. With that being said, in my teenages I was mainly PvP minded and for me PvP was interesting enough by itself. In contrast to PvE, each fight is so much different due to the mixtures of classes/players/playstyles. Yes, there have been plenty of overpowered classes over time, but eventually you ill find some way to deal with it. The only reason I don't do as much PvP is because of hardcore mode.

Even after rereading your post, I still find it difficult to comprehend what you exactly need more from PvP than what it currently is, but maybe that is my shortcoming.
 - Does more type of battleground or arena fix it? --> Same fights, different background?
 - PvP gear equivalent to PvE gear? --> Will attract noobs to PvP which you can slaugther?
 - Better class balance? --> Risk of loss of identity?

The only change that I could think of to boost PvP is allowing hardcores to reserruct in battlegrounds.

## Post 156769 by Cheruscan (Bug Report Enthusiast) — 2025-10-23T21:07:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=156769#p156769 | page 4 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Akarui wrote: Thu Oct 23, 2025 8:39 pm**
> holy bullsht. wtf tauren priests, its so disgusting. How and when cows that lived in harmony with nature, started to think about holy light? taurens revere spirits and ancestors in their own way. And not like the night elves and Elune. What next. orc paladin? h.e. shaman?

They didnt, priest class in lore is an amalgum of different subclasses that are race bound. Ofc ingame this wouldnt be possible so everyone gets the same talent trees. You have to use your imagination, otherwise Vanilla nelf/troll/undead priests make no sense already.

The harmony with nature part is well and good but essentially there are no classes representing that for the Tauren. Shamans commune with the elements, so unless you view the earthmother worship as rock elemental worship these cant be their religious leaders.

Druids are a retcon to make the class available to both factions, they should be nightelf exclusive.

Priest is by far the most sensible class to add for the taurens.

Again they dont worship the holy light, but they do have to conform to the gameplay limitations, we will see how turtle will shape the actual beliefsystem of the taurens. I am excited   happy_turtle_head

> **Solveit wrote: Fri Oct 03, 2025 11:43 am**
> > **Turboman wrote: Fri Oct 03, 2025 11:10 am**
> > > **Solveit wrote: Fri Oct 03, 2025 10:38 am**
> > > You have entire ally teams skipping around like dipshits with permanent freedom on the flag carrier as soon as they hit a critical mass of 3 paladins and virtually nobody is so priest stacked horde side that they can assume the full role of managing paladin buff spam.
> > >
> > >  For frick's sake fix it.
> >
> >   Did you really made a post about you being mad that alliance players can use their buttons now? Instead of them, you know, being instantly purged by 5 fucking shamans in the horde team plus a couple of priests? Purge spam was so bad to the point that alliance players didn't even bother buffing themselves before the match or at the gy. You are the only dipshit here as far as i'm concerned. Hopefully they will make cd even longer.
>
>   Wow, pal. sOrRy your little pve rotations get messed up when your opponents have answers to your immunity buffs in pvp.
>
>  People like you should stick to playing vs the environment imo, where you can have immunity abilities without counters and it's not a balancing issue in any particular regard. They can give the boss a way to dispell your buffs only every 15 seconds or whatever you want, and you can tell yourselves you're actually good players for timing your immunity cd in the 15 second window, or whatever duning kruger type exercise you need to do for your own ego.

Let me inform you of a couple of things first. Bubble is a 5-minute CD, and in bloodring they always die because they get killed before their global CD lets them use Bubble (greed is a sin). Let them bubble. Also, the blessing of freedom is not for the Paladin; it's really op on all other classes, but the Paladin, and people who will use freedom to the maximum effectiveness are few. I play 2 rogue ally and horde 60, a druid horde, and I have a paladin that I use to farm sometimes. I do 1 bg. What I learn from playing Paladin is that Rogue counters Paladin so hard. If you can trinket their stun and have evasion, if you don't just gauge him, everything a ret does can be dodged. Paladins always get out of combat too so easy to sap them. What op right now is full biss holy who never die, it's really the power house ret is even full biss not even close of a survival hunter.

## Post 166378 by daleking0518 — 2026-01-25T03:54:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=166378#p166378 | page 9 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

We urge you to abandon the Felhunter rework plan and even completely rework the class balance scheme for Warlocks. The player base of Warlocks is already dwindling, and their performance in PvP is far from satisfactory. Such adjustments will only drive more players away, potentially leading to the class's total extinction.

Class balance cannot be judged one-sidedly. A class excelling in a certain field does not necessarily mean it is overpowered—it may stem from specific gear or in-depth mastery by an extremely small number of players. These are isolated cases; most players just follow the meta when seeing a class as strong, without devoting much time to in-depth research on any single class.

Please check the in-game activity statistics and see how many players are still willing to main Warlock. Just like Mages nowadays, their player base is shrinking constantly. For a class that is already difficult to master, such changes will only push it into complete obscurity.

## Post 167504 by Heretic1987 — 2026-02-05T09:59:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=167504#p167504 | page 9 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

A few thoughts on the changes to Rogue in 1.18. As i see it rogues overall have unlike all other classes only one strength - single target dps (aside from small exceptions like Suppression room in BWL, or an interrupt which other classes can do as well). All other classes have different utilities, buffs, AoE and so on but rogues only purpose most of the time is single target dps and therefor they are and should remain best at it.

There may be an argument for a small nerf to Assassination dmg but the nerf as it is outlined now is a bit too much. First of all, Assassination is not the best performing spec. Combat daggers perform better once you have AQ40/Naxx gear. Assassination is just a very good stepping stone. Its a reliable way for a rogue to earn a place in the raid by the only way we can - single target dps. Rogues dps being so much higher then other classes is a way to compensate for their lack of other utility in most cases making them worth including into the raid. Subtelty support spec is indeed viable regarding overall utility in raid but the dmg nerf could force every rogue to go Subtelty and make other specs less usefull.

Second point is that the nerf alters the gameplay and makes it less engaging. A 5% nerf to Noxious assault and 1% per combo point to Taste for blood is fair and doesnt affect the gameplay. What does affect the gameplay is the change to Taste for blood, making it no longer affect Dissolvent poison.
It both nerfs the overall dmg too much (i would argue the % to Noxx and TfB is enough as it is) but also makes Taste for blood much less useful. Big part of what made the Assassination spec so engaging is the need to keep up Slice&Dice, Envenom and as high Taste for Blood as you can. How good you where at it decided your overall DPS as rogue. I myself was often able to edge out better geared rogues by paying attention to this rotation and it is both engaging and rewarding to do so. If you change Taste for blood to no longer affect Dissolvent poison at all you loose a big part of that aspect of gameplay. Keeping up Taste for blood as one of the 3 skills will have much less of an impact to overall dmg and could cause many ppl to skip it altogether, reducing gameplay rotation and engagement with combat.

By reducing the scaling of Noxious assault from 35% to 30% and the physical dmg buff of Taste for blood from 3% to 2% per combo point you already reduce the overall dmg of rogues quite significantly. I would argue that changing Taste for blood to no longer affect dissolvent poison will reduce the damage far enough so that other dps classes will be more preferable to bring into raids instead of rogues (why bring a rogue if a mage has same dps or close enough + can AoE, sheep and so on) and also make the gameplay less engaging and rewarding.

P.S. And with coming buffs to BWL we will need all the dmg we can get :)

## Post 167512 by Cheruscan (Bug Report Enthusiast) — 2026-02-05T12:08:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=167512#p167512 | page 9 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Heretic1987 wrote: Thu Feb 05, 2026 9:59 am**
> A few thoughts on the changes to Rogue in 1.18. As i see it rogues overall have unlike all other classes only one strength - single target dps (aside from small exceptions like Suppression room in BWL, or an interrupt which other classes can do as well). All other classes have different utilities, buffs, AoE and so on but rogues only purpose most of the time is single target dps and therefor they are and should remain best at it.
>
>  There may be an argument for a small nerf to Assassination dmg but the nerf as it is outlined now is a bit too much. First of all, Assassination is not the best performing spec. Combat daggers perform better once you have AQ40/Naxx gear. Assassination is just a very good stepping stone. Its a reliable way for a rogue to earn a place in the raid by the only way we can - single target dps. Rogues dps being so much higher then other classes is a way to compensate for their lack of other utility in most cases making them worth including into the raid. Subtelty support spec is indeed viable regarding overall utility in raid but the dmg nerf could force every rogue to go Subtelty and make other specs less usefull.
>
>  Second point is that the nerf alters the gameplay and makes it less engaging. A 5% nerf to Noxious assault and 1% per combo point to Taste for blood is fair and doesnt affect the gameplay. What does affect the gameplay is the change to Taste for blood, making it no longer affect Dissolvent poison.
>  It both nerfs the overall dmg too much (i would argue the % to Noxx and TfB is enough as it is) but also makes Taste for blood much less useful. Big part of what made the Assassination spec so engaging is the need to keep up Slice&Dice, Envenom and as high Taste for Blood as you can. How good you where at it decided your overall DPS as rogue. I myself was often able to edge out better geared rogues by paying attention to this rotation and it is both engaging and rewarding to do so. If you change Taste for blood to no longer affect Dissolvent poison at all you loose a big part of that aspect of gameplay. Keeping up Taste for blood as one of the 3 skills will have much less of an impact to overall dmg and could cause many ppl to skip it altogether, reducing gameplay rotation and engagement with combat.
>
>  By reducing the scaling of Noxious assault from 35% to 30% and the physical dmg buff of Taste for blood from 3% to 2% per combo point you already reduce the overall dmg of rogues quite significantly. I would argue that changing Taste for blood to no longer affect dissolvent poison will reduce the damage far enough so that other dps classes will be more preferable to bring into raids instead of rogues (why bring a rogue if a mage has same dps or close enough + can AoE, sheep and so on) and also make the gameplay less engaging and rewarding.
>
>  P.S. And with coming buffs to BWL we will need all the dmg we can get :)

Slice and dice gives you an easy way to hit a second target consistantly when needed, so rogue isnt, single target only, rogue also gets 2 threat reduction skills, other classes get only 1 or none, not to mention that evasion can temporarily turn you into a tank that takes no dmg. manipulating patrols with distract is another trick that only rogues have AND unlike all the utility other classes might bring its actually useful in raids (slows, sheep, etc either do not work or are not used 98% of the time in raids).

Being the highest dps just means sweat groups replaced their warrior execute squad with rogues, but the class will remain important even with reduced dps.

Rogue nerfs will just led to normalization as of rn rogues make up the majority of melee dps in raids I attend.

## Post 167515 by Heretic1987 — 2026-02-05T12:42:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=167515#p167515 | page 9 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

I think you mean Blade flurry and while it can be good for solofarm sometimes its not very usefull in raids tbh. Its not AoE because you only hit one extra target so the usecases are limited. When there are alot of small mobs to AoE, hitting one extra target is not enough. And when there are a few big mobs they are often tanked too far apart for it to reach. Even when you can use it to hit two targets effectively its risky, tank focuses on the skull and you risk ripping threat on the second target that you are hitting. It also doesnt work with Assassination because poison procs are not transferred to the second target. In any case, nobody brings a rogue for the AoE.

You are correct, rogues have 2 threat reduction skills and i would argue that it is so for the very reason that single target dps is the primary goal. Those threat reduction skills are the necessary tools to able to let it rip. No point in having best dps if cant use it, right? Two threat reduction skills only proves the point that rogues purpose is single target dps.

Evasion is great but its mostly a way to save your own ass if you messed up and aggroed. Cant imagine too many raids bring a rogue as an extra tank. Warriors on the other hand, even dps warriors can and often do OT, pick up mobs etc. For ex in MC warr and druid dps would be preferable to rogues because of both Garr and Majordomo fights if they have similar dps anyway. So unlike rogues they always do have a second utility to them in raids. Not to mention Battle shout, 200AP is huge!

Distract is also very situational. In all my raids it is only truly needed in two spots - suppression room in BWL and near Hakkar in ZG and only because pathing for the mobbs bugged out and two packs got on top of each other.

And as i said, a small nerf is not unreasonable. I do think that the outlined % nerf to Noxious assault and Taste for blood is fair, it will only affect the dmg but not the gameplay or the rotations. Making Taste for blood no longer affect Dissolvent poison might nerf overall dmg too much though and even worse - alter the gameplay. If Taste for blood no longer makes a meaningful difference to dps for Assassination there is no longer any point to keep it up in the rotation, so its just dumbs down the gameplay.

We will see after patch.

## Post 167523 by Heretic1987 — 2026-02-05T14:59:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=167523#p167523 | page 9 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

I wont argue that distract isnt useful in Naxx but its only relevant for a small % of rogues, many still havent even set foot there. And in any case, biggest nerf is to Assassination and when you get to Naxx combat dagger outperforms it already (but receives a much smaller nerf). I get nerfing the best spec, but why nerf something thats already second best?

In most cases ive seen the skill of the rogue is not the issue, the biggest issue is the knowledge and understanding of the class. Ive seen Assassination rogues use 2.90 MH weapons, or only focusing on %speed and loosing out on other stats, or just not speccing into Taste for blood and the resulting dmg suffers no matter what gear they have.

With 3 points in Ruthlesness its really easy to keep Slice & Dice and Envenom up. Keeping up Rupture for the Taste for blood buff as well isnt making it much harder skill-wise. What it does is stop the rotation from feeling braindead. Suddenly the number of combopoints to use with Rupture matters, how much HP the mob has, or how many mobs are left, or when the next pull is. So to optimize dmg you need to pay at least a little attention when to use Rupture and when to use Eviscerate. And if you do you get rewarded with better dps making it actually engaging.

In Assassination 50% of the dmg comes from poison so if Taste for blood no longer affects Disslovent poison dmg as well as being reduced in dmg% then it might no longer be significant enough to reward the player for paying attention and we are back to mindless button mashing rotation.

Out of curiosity, the one rogue you mention, what spec does he use and whats his overall and peak dps in a raid?

## Post 167885 by Heretic1987 — 2026-02-10T13:40:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=167885#p167885 | page 9 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Cheruscan wrote: Tue Feb 10, 2026 12:54 pm**
> > **Heretic1987 wrote: Tue Feb 10, 2026 12:42 pm**
> > > **Cheruscan wrote: Tue Feb 10, 2026 11:14 am**
> > > You should have quoted me, I only stumbled upon you post by accident, rechecking this threat.
> > >
> > >  anyway
> > >
> > >  <https://turtlogs.com/viewer/81435/base?history_state=1>
> > >
> > >  about 1k - 1,1k average, peaks you can check in the logs, its Nuhaloossi. Hes assasination specc ofc
> >
> >   Ah, wasnt sure if the  quote was needed for a ping.
> >
> >  Anyway, according to your link Nuhaloossi is Combat daggers, not Assassination. In fact all the rogues in that Kara raid where running Combat daggers and thats exactly my point - once you get to Naxx, combat daggers becomes the strongest spec anyway. I know that it gets a small nerf too but Assassination gets a much bigger nerf.
>
>   Oh sry ofc I am stupid xD

All the rogues average about 700dps in that raid and Nuhaloossi is 1100dps so we have both the baseline and the potential of Combat daggers in current patch show here.

Now lets compare it to Assassiantion as it is right now. I have an optimal build with basically no major upgrades until Naxx and on a very good day i will average around 700 overall dps at the end of the raid. Thats with all the buffs and full focus to maintain the most optimal rotation. Doing so puts me ahead of most other rogues running Assassination so its more or less as good as it gets.

And now lets look at the coming nerf. -5% AP scaling on Noxious attack, not too bad. -1% dmg buff per combo point from Taste for blood - getting worse but still more or less tolerable. Taste for blood no longer affecting Dissolvent poison (and its literally what makes 50% of your overall dmg) - now we are way past the line.

That nerf will cost me somewhere around 150dps or more. Note that Combat daggers are only getting a nerf of 5% to Opportunity so at 700dps you loose about 35dps. The result is that you loose the progression curve.
As it is now you can get around 300dps as a fresh Assassination rogue with pre-raid BiS. Then you slowly progress to 700dps as you gear up through MC/BWL/AQ and when you hit Naxx-gear you can progress past 700dps using Combat daggers.
If nerf goes through with no changes i doubt that you will be able to pass 500dps with Assassination no matter the gear you have (it doesn't scale with weapon dmg so only real scaling is AP and its limited). Only once you get to Naxx you will be able to jump to 700+ using Combat daggers.

P.S. All my dps-measurements are in regard to the overall dps at the end of the raid. Ofc its possible to peak much more on the certain boss where you can go all out and get the full rotation going.

>

The new PvP reward progression, which was meant to be the highlight for PvP in the Nightmares of Ursol, required significant itemization changes, took far longer than we expected, and we still haven't implemented a way to obtain all of those rewards.
>
>  Alongside that, this patch introduces Thorn Gorge, a new battleground, new world‑PvP events, and a few other changes such as arena‑consume restrictions and a PvP dampening (see the class‑changes changelog).

Hi Torta, the patchnotes still miss the most requested wpvp feature from the Tel'abim community:

Faction leader loot.

This has been requested by TA players since the beginning of 2024, ie. the first months of the server. It was on the roadmap at one point but went missing. Now faction leader heads are in the game but give no rewards/have no quests connected to them. Can you please just 1) add a connected quest with a suitable reward (and dragonslayer-like city buff), 2) buff the faction leaders slightly? Currently, they are not much of a challenge unless defended.

Moreover, a wpvp-related challenge would provide great, long-term content with little coding required. My suggestion:

 5000 (or similar number) wpvp kills gives the player a title - let's say 'Hero of the Horde/Alliance' - and a banner that is identic to the rank9 banner but works in world. Done. Only limitations would be to limit "kills" to honor-generating kills (naturally) and max ~3 kills per opposing player per 24 hours to prevent wintrading.

I promise you, these additions would satisfy much of the pvp crowd on the TA server.

On the topic of BG-related pvp I will add that dampening has not solved any of the balance issues in pvp. The class/spec-based dampening you are considering now will not do so either. You will unfortunately have to look at class performance more seriously.

## Post 168552 by Cysthen (Patch Note Conspiracy Theorist) — 2026-02-17T10:02:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=168552#p168552 | page 10 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

Before everyone goes crazy remember they wrote PVP Item Changes on the Item Changelog and wrote there are Rewards for the WPVP Events. For all we know it might be more than we think.

As far as how to introduce improved item sets I had a thought that seemed to catch a lot of PVP Players attention and the short of it is to get rid of Group Que altogether and have everyone have their own Personal Rating for ALL BATTLEGROUNDS combined. Consumes, and World Buffs also disabled in Battlegrounds. Perhaps some Consumes debatable.

It would work a lot like Splatoon and a lot of multiplayer games currently for example where your MMR is just your overall win rate no matter what. It helps remove the AFK Player problem because everyone will actually care about winning more and teams will remain random due to Cross Faction and only Solo Que being available. Personal Rating could be Reset every 2 Weeks if you go negative at a NPC.

Arenas I think in the current state would be harder to make competitive and are slightly less in the spirit of Vanilla as bursty unbalanced PVP is kind of what Vanilla is in general. However if you wanted to keep Arenas important with Group Que it could have Group Que enabled only for seperate Ranked Ques and normal Skirmish would be limited to Solo Que and apply to your Personal Rating like the rest of them.

This Personal Rating could be required for new improved PVP Item Sets that are slightly less power than K40 Gear, we recently made a PVP Council Discord and are coming up with item set ideas and they plan to make a post similar to my Lunatic Gear Suggestion Post eventually. The idea is loosely upgraded sets with the same R14 Models that has a SpellVisualID to differentiate it so it's less work and more existing assets.

*Last edited by Cysthen on Tue Feb 17, 2026 11:35 am, edited 1 time in total.*

## Post 168568 by Phoenixphire (Barrens Chat Casualty) — 2026-02-17T11:41:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=168568#p168568 | page 10 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Animejesus wrote: Tue Feb 17, 2026 10:40 am**
> reducing the exp bonus now seems really unfair

Idk about unfair.. it def feels bad, but its not an all together bad change imo

The reason classic takes so long to level for the most part besides the shear XP requirements is the lack of good quests in multiple level brackets and zones.

Twow has fixed that 3x over. The issue tents create is because they are SO good:

 1. New players already don't see much of the new content early on because they can just do their usual leveling route with permanent rested. They almost need to seek out the new content because with permanent rested they can just avoid the new content. (For example I know people who tried Twow for the first time and leveled to 30 barely doing any new content) Because why would they if they can just do what they are used to with permanent rested?
 2. It makes you feel like its TOO efficient not to sit under a tent to 150% everytime you run out. At least with 25% rested and the fact it gets used up slower because its 50% increased XP instead of 100%. It isn't as big of a deal if you don't have constant rested because the tents are much less of a big deal.
 3. The constant rested will make you out level early zones fairly consistently, once again making it so if the Twow team wants to add quests later to a 1-10 zone or 10-20 zone. Most likely the player will quickly outlevel a zone before making it to that later zone quest and never see it. A really good example is Tirisfal Uplands, the elite quest at the end gives an INSANE 2hand sword for the level, but you never see people with it because most people out level the zone so fast they never get to that quest and they just leave to go to Ashenvale or w.e.

This is one of those "Taking the toys away" moments that is going to feel bad for players. And maybe it needs tweaking to make it less of a nerf. Who knows until people get their hands on the new survival profession.

But as much as people love them and it is cool to see everyone hanging out under them, Tents were healthy for Twow when it didn't have 2000+ new quests, 5 new leveling dungeons, many new leveling areas and zones, like 8 or whatever leveling dungeon extensions. On top of that you have new race class combos to try, new professions and new profession items to discover, and new class changes to try out. Because back then, there wasn't enough new content to expect every player to level to 60 at 1x. At this point it speeds up the leveling to a point the devs obviously aren't happy with, and makes players feel like every level and a half they have to run back to Org and sit under a tent afk for 10 minutes, taking them out of the world.

Lets see how survival is when it comes out, but so far I don't hate this change personally. But I understand it will 100% upset people. "Taking the toys away" will always piss people off as a developer. Maybe this is way to far and will get reverted, we will have to see how it plays when the patch comes out. But my personal opinion is tents are too good as they currently are.

*Last edited by Phoenixphire on Tue Feb 17, 2026 12:03 pm, edited 1 time in total.*

> Tents & Taverns
>
>  Both taverns and tents now grant the rested XP bonus at the same pace.
>
>  Rested XP from killing creatures was reduced to a 50% bonus instead of the previous 100% bonus.
>
>  Tent's rested XP bonus was capped at 25% of a player's rested XP, tents provide no benefit if the player has more than 25% rested XP.
>
>  All taverns except those located in cities can now raise a player's rested XP up to 100%.

This is the craziest thing I've seen and a very disappointing addition to what I feel is an otherwise good patch. One of the big reasons this server is so attractive is because of the leveling speed. Nothing is stopping people from not using tents or taking challenges that remove that aspect entirely. All this actively does is harm the reputation of the server and alienate a part of your core audience with nothing to show in return. It doesn't improve the game in any way by adding anything, all it does is remove an entire group of people who just want to play Turtle the way it has been for years.

If leveling time is forced to be artificially slower than the server standard that's been set, it's a deal breaker for any alt making. Less alts, less players to do dungeons, more "ded server", less players period. I'd urge you to think before you act with this one.

## Post 168639 by Torta (Turtle WoW Team) [STAFF] — 2026-02-17T19:26:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=168639#p168639 | page 11 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

To address concerns about the nerfed rested XP. We weren't happy with two things:

- Logically, a cozy tavern feels way better for a break than a tent out in the woods.

- The tents at Stormwind and Orgrimmar's gates have been a mess for ages, with too many players camping together.
So the fix isn't really a nerf but a a change in how it works. Instead of rushing to a capital city and squeezing under a tent with a hundred other folks, you can now get the same rested XP bonus wherever there's a tavern in the world. The speed of earning rested XP in a tavern is about the same as it was under a tent before 1.18.1.

Note that none of this affects rested‑bonus gain in taverns while the player is offline.

Tents in 1.18.1 will act like the new taverns but won't give you the full rested bonus (can't feel fully rested unless you're comfortable).

## Post 168649 by Torta (Turtle WoW Team) [STAFF] — 2026-02-17T20:13:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=168649#p168649 | page 11 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Hctwowfan wrote: Tue Feb 17, 2026 7:56 pm**
> Fine with that. Tents outside SW/OG were stupid. How does that warrant a -50% bonus kill XP nerf tho?

We were in a tough spot with this one. The overly OP tents have been in the game for so long, and we knew that if we removed them, some people would probably travel to our home countries to personally express their disappointment with a bit of kung‑fu. So we chose to at least preserve immersion with our changes: make the rested bonus more accessible worldwide and revitalize the taverns, but nerf rested bonus actual value so XP rate isn't permanently doubled in the game.

## Post 168694 by Rangtuk — 2026-02-18T03:18:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=168694#p168694 | page 11 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

As it stands, I don't like the proposed tent changes. As somebody actively RPing with <The Morrowhearth>, I would feel extremely disappointed if their niche was suddenly rendered near useless. I have a few suggestions that I think would improve tents, whilst still keeping taverns as a main resting point.

Tavern and tent speeds should not be equal.
I will grant that it makes sense realistically to feel more rested after a night at the tavern, compared to roughing it in the wilderness. However I think this can be accomplished with a speed variance rather than capping tent exp. Making tents provide rested at a moderately slower rate will still keep taverns as the go-to if you want to quickly get back in to levelling, however if you'd like to mob grind somewhere for a while (or sit down and chat with folks), then the tent is an option to keep you from travelling back to a town.
This also counterbalances with the ability to place tents "anywhere", freedom of placement but slow gains vs. specific locations and quicker gains.

If you're going to cap rested gain via tents (which I dislike), then there should be another incentive to bother with carrying around tent mats and a long cooldown.
Just making it 25% more rested exp before you're stuck going back to a town isn't going to mean much, especially if the tent mats stay the same. Levellers past 15 have to go out of their way to collect linen.
I propose adding some small stat buff for sitting under a tent, make it last 1-2 hours (as long as the tent's cooldown, perhaps have it dependent on how long you're under the tent, like the Midsummer Ribbon dance buff in Retail).

Providing a stat buff would fit also fit mechanically, every other profession has some sort of upgrading value. Armor kits, gear, bandages, belt buckles, cooking, etc. they don't just have "one-and-done" items for every level bracket.
If you want to turn Survival into a proper 5-60 profession, then making better tents that provide better bonuses/speed is a very easy way to do this. This will allow you to have higher material requirements (wool, silk, mageweave, runecloth) for the levellers who readily access those materials, as well as pairing nicely with higher level lumber (as you mentioned wanting to pair Woodworking/cutting with Survival).
This also gives a very simple way to provide new tent models, which would be extremely beneficial for both world flavor and the RP community.

You can even go further with this concept, having multiple tent tiers means you can also give unique tent recipes (and models) to reputations.
The basic tent recipes can give some amount of basic stats (str, agi, sta, int, spi) proportional to their level bracket, they'll start as the red/blue lean-tos that we have now, and could upgrade with bigger (or fancier) models for each next tier.
For max level, you can provide special tent buffs. For example:
Cenarion Tree Tent (Purplish/Night Elven tarps lashed between two small saplings). Provides Nature Resistance and Haste.
Furbolg Log Tent (Log with the tarp seen in many furbolg camps). Provides Stamina and additional nature damage on hits.

Will high-tier raiders be incentivized to min-max these? Yes probably, but they are a bonus not an expectation, they're also tied to a secondary profession thus everyone can use them. As well as they'll have to return to the open world if they want to refresh their buffs.
(High-tier raiders will most definitely be incentivized to use the trap system you teased anyways, but I wont go into that as this is supposed to be about tents, not Survival wholely)

You could provide this buff to taverns as well, perhaps with a longer duration. Again, keeping taverns stronger, while leaving tents some "stake" in the game, ba dum tish.

## Post 168706 by Hctwowfan (Bug Report Enthusiast) — 2026-02-18T07:55:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=168706#p168706 | page 11 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Grizb37 wrote: Wed Feb 18, 2026 2:41 am**
> > **Hctwowfan wrote: Wed Feb 18, 2026 1:16 am**
> > > **Grizb37 wrote: Wed Feb 18, 2026 12:13 am**
> > > I dont get why people are complaining about the tents?
> > >
> > >  You can now get the same Rested XP as tents but in an INN? So you dont have to wait for some random 60 to drop one.
> > >
> > >  This is a buff not a nerf lmao
> >
> >   You do realize that you're currently getting +100% XP for killing a mob while rested and that this will be reduced to only giving +50%, right?
>
>   Fine with me, I thought the +100% was a bit too fast, I was outlvling most of my quests while I killed the mobs needed for them, 0% was abit too slow sometimes, but 50% sounds like a great middle ground!, Also I wont have to wait for a 60 to drop a tent and can just use the inns.
>
>  Vanilla was never designed to have a permanent rested EXP.

Ok so I guess we can agree that it is a nerf and not a buff.

For the earliest version of vanilla wow, the devs wanted to implement an XP penalty for players that leveled too fast. If it had come to fruition, a player could receive a certain amount of normal XP per day and receive an XP modifier that turned XP gain down to 10% of normal XP if that daily amount was reached. The devs soon realized that an XP penalty would not sit right with the players and instead reversed the concept. You now receive normal XP indefinitely but a player that does not play and instead parks their toon in an inn receives bonus XP for a set amount of time (or rather xp threshold depending on their level) as catch up mechanic. Sitting in an inn for 3 days or sitting anywhere for 10 days while logged out gives the 150% rested XP bonus. It's the same earlier concept but reversed. The bonus XP was +100% for mob kills.

It takes rather long to get fully rested but you get 2x kill XP while being rested.

Turtle wow now decides that the concept should be that getting rested is piss easy (no change here to turtle wow before changes) but that bonus XP is only +50%, turning rested XP from a catch up mechanic to ... well whatever the point is now when you can get fully rested while getting a coffee.

It's a more than 20 yo game. Near everyone playing vanilla is playing it for nostalgia reasons and has played and leveled 20 years ago.

People a) decided to play on turtle partly based on the de facto 2x leveling speed and b) get used to their candy now taken away. I understand why this sudden change (out of nowhere) upsets people.

I dont think the average player will feel any difference, besides with Survival becoming a fully fledged secondary profession (that seemingly acts as a mix of alchemi/engineering light) you will almost never be without a tent.

people that do challenges also have no leg to stand on here, you are literally doing it to yourself  sad_turtle_head  if you cant accept the challenge is actually challenging than just stop, Devs could give a 2 week period where GMs will remove challenges from players if they ask or have an NPC added to do it (whichever works better)

We dont know everything that will be included in the survival profession yet ofc, so if devs havent included different "tiers" of tent they should add it in.

(they dont have to be any different from the basic tent, its just more convenient to use materials that are readily available to the player rather than having to carry a stack of low lvl materials with you at all times. Tent placement should be restricted to prevent griefing, towns that have a working inn should prevent tent placement in a certain radius around them, smaller outposts without an inn (Refugee point, Morgans Vigil should either have a specific spot to be "tented at" (could be marked by a special campfire) or just have a very small radius around them that prevents tent placement

the area immediatly outside capitols should also prevent tent placement, especially on the road

## Post 168715 by Atreidon (Grandmaster of Forum PvP) — 2026-02-18T09:00:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=168715#p168715 | page 12 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

The rested nerf will slow players down, but i think many of you blow it out of proportion. If any inn now gives you rested up to 100%, it means every level you have to sit in any inn for ~15mins. Thats something that most players do on accident just idling after a hearthstone here and there.

As for the nerf in rested amount, you loose 1/4 of mob exp while rested. Going from 200% to 150%.

For people actually questing, the quest reward exp dont change, so you can expect to lvl about 85% of the previous rested speed.

Part of which is counteracted by any inn now working for rested. Reducing the required frequecy of visiting the tent slum in front of og or sw.

That being said, if tents are reduced in usefulness, can we please remove or at least reduce the 2h cd on them?
Its just unnecessarily prohibitive and if tents now get pushed into the role they should have had all along, a quick shelter for a break, or scenery for some impromtu rp whilechilling, the cd on both create and deploy always made you wanna minmax ita usefulness.

## Post 168719 by Xudo (Grandmaster of Forum PvP) — 2026-02-18T09:28:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=168719#p168719 | page 12 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Cheruscan wrote: Wed Feb 18, 2026 8:25 am**
> We dont know everything that will be included in the survival profession yet ofc, so if devs havent included different "tiers" of tent they should add it in.
>
>  (they dont have to be any different from the basic tent, its just more convenient to use materials that are readily available to the player rather than having to carry a stack of low lvl materials with you at all times. It also makes little sense, Survival is about doing a bear grylls and crafting smth useful out in the field with whatever you have available)
>
>  Different tent Tiers could then just have different visuals to add to RP (but this can be patched in later no rush)
>
>  Tent placement should be restricted to prevent griefing, towns that have a working inn should prevent tent placement in a certain radius around them, smaller outposts without an inn (Refugee point, Morgans Vigil should either have a specific spot to be "tented at" (could be marked by a special campfire) or just have a very small radius around them that prevents tent placement
>
>  the area immediatly outside capitols should also prevent tent placement, especially on the road

All those suggestions are cool, but they are too convoluted.
Linen cloth for single tier of tent works just fine. It also supports low level economy in terms that high level characters usually just buy that cloth off AH. It allows to transfer money to support low levels.

Restriction to placement is most convoluted thing. The fact that tents will be worse than inn will automatically remove all tents in front of capitals. Because whole capital gives rested xp as inn. So it will be meaningless to place tents near entrance.
Additional restrictions to roads is super hard from technical perspective. How would you differentiate the road from any other place in the world?
I actually don't see any examples of spells that *can't* be cast in certain areas, except roots under roofs.
It would be easier to define areas where tents can be placed instead. It will be similar to "blacksmiths can create stuff near anvil". But then again, you have to populate whole world with those places. I mean - you need to choose the spot without road, without mobs in aggro radius, far enough from nearest settlement. Considering how huge the world is, it will be a lot of work.
In the end, you should somehow tell players "you can place tent here".
Campfire is not the solution you would like. Because players can put campfires anywhere with no restrictions. So they will workaround the core idea of placement restriction.
Afterall, wow is not a game about placing tents. I don't see reason to add convoluted rules regarding them.

> **Sylveria wrote: Wed Feb 18, 2026 8:33 am**
> I assume that the Cooldown of Tents will also be lowered with the nerf? That was people can still decided for themselves If they Go To a tavern, or If they Set Up a tent in the wilds
>  Like 30min CD? Filling Up 25% Rested, then going on with the Business as usual. That way i think Tents will really be used Out in the wilds Instead of Stormwind/Orgrimmar/Goldshire.. the usual spots.

I agree that with nerf of usefulness of tents, their cd should be lowered too.
If previous cap of rested xp was 150% and tents reduce it to 25%, then CD can be reduced in 150/25=6 times. From 2 hours to 20 minutes.

## Post 168749 by Stonedj33sus — 2026-02-18T12:47:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=168749#p168749 | page 12 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

I do not agree with nerfing the rested XP from mobs. It is a very overkill nerf imo. I could perhaps understand lowering it 25% but 50 is way too much. It affects players more than you think.

Inns are more accessible yes but that does not warrant the nerf, since tents are still very very accessible at this moment. There’s always tents in front of capitals. This does not change accessibility by much. And like people mentioned, if you want to go slow and steady, there is already that option. Or just make rested XP togglable, so people can play how they want.

I already see many people being discouraged from leveling alts now and it just boosts the idea of “go AoE class and just farm mobs”. This is not what classic gameplay should be, turning it into retail. And this is also a nerf for people who do not have much time to play and catch up on content…

Please reconsider this devs. The majority of the community does not like this decision. Please listen to the players.

The leveling rate is one of the biggest reasons I like TurtleWoW.

## Post 168957 by Venaven — 2026-02-19T22:05:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=168957#p168957 | page 12 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

>wait 4+ months for what's supposed to be "the pvp update" after the previous update delayed it
>it's delayed again
>can't wait to wait another year only for it to probably be delayed again at this rate
Look, I get that Turtle started out as a PvE server, but the server's life has grown so immensely over the years that it would be naive at this point to continue shunning a portion of your playerbase which is no longer trivial in scope. In addition to the PvPers playing on the PvE servers, we can also account for the fact that, against all odds, Tel'Abim experienced some long-term growth, but all of that is about to decline. The reality is that there is a decent amount of interest in PvP and a community yearning for it, but if they are continually placed on the backburner, animosity grows and, in a self-fulfilling prophecy, the playerbase will dwindle due to the burnout of their collective faith. This is plainly disappointing. And then the PvE side isn't happy either, because of the tent changes. Turtle is capable of producing great content but a lot of the times their priorities seem rather awkward relative to what the player demand is. I will say that the changes made to world bosses this patch are excellent, though, just so that people know I can still appreciate what the devs do... insofar as they actually *do it*.
There's tons of PvE content and PvErs have shown that they're pretty happy to just raidlog the same content over and over again until the end of time, but PvP is in some serious need of intervention in order to be playable.
