## Post 169218 by Spacepony — 2026-02-22T19:42:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=169218#p169218 | page 13 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

I feel like the community sometimes talks like dampening will be class/spec reductions on damage/healing *done*, and sometimes talks like dampening will be class/spec reductions on damage/healing *taken*.  And it’s not clear to me from the patch notes which form of dampening is being considered.  In any 1v1 matchup, it may seem like tuning damage done can get the same result as tuning damage taken.  But because Turtle has more than 2 classes, these two forms of dampening aren’t equivalent.

Suppose (for example), a need arises is to make rogues tankier with no spillover effects on other class matchups not involving rogues.  When dampening reduces damage done by class/spec, reducing all non-rogue damage in order to make rogues tankier would have clear spillover effects on other class matchups.  And also not make rogues tankier versus other rogues.  Now suppose we have the second form of dampening that instead reduces damage taken by class/spec.  Then rogues could be made tankier with no spillover effects on other class matchups.

But this second form of dampening that reduces damage taken could also be worse, depending on what needs adjusting:  If the need arises say to reduce rogue damage, reducing the damage taken by all non-rogues would again have spillovers in other class matchups.  IMO, Turtle may need both per class/spec tuning of damage/healing done AND per class/spec tuning of damage/healing taken.  Now the vast majority of classes/specs may share some default values for all these dampening values.  But for those few tricky-to-balance specs, I think fine tuning of both these flavors of dampening will be needed.

## Post 169572 by Gerganon — 2026-02-28T06:31:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=169572#p169572 | page 13 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

Comments on the proposed priest changes, from a dedicated, pvp focused, solo-player.

I'm 18/11/22, a rarely seen hybrid spec, which will no longer be possible if the proposed changes make it live.  I'm very dismayed by this.

I've mostly pvp gear equipped, and as a result my undead-priest racial ability costs a full 1/4 of my mana.  I feel inner focus is mandatory because they share the same cooldown and without it, Devouring Plague simply costs way too much mana.  Especially with hunters spamming such a low-risk, high-reward ability like viper sting all the time (separate issue).

I'm 11 in holy because holy nova is a mandatory pvp ability.  Catching rogues and ferals (and even shadowmelded defenders) can be a priest's only path to a win condition for a majority of fights vs. Stealthies.

Finally, 22 in shadow because rank 1 mind flay, improved mana burn, and silence are all staples of pvp and feel completely mandatory as well.

If the proposed changes go through, my priest will have to choose between reduced cast on mana burn and will lose access to blackout stun (because there are way too many mandatory talents competing on the same tier now).

Lastly and maybe most importantly, lightwell is the only thing that allows me to solo farm content that other classes find completely trivial.  The new lightwell will provide such little benefit to me when defending a gank because of how tw changed cast bar pushback (seems there are no diminishing returns on pushback, and even with 50% on mind blast, and 70% on holy spells, it is very common to double or even triple a spells cast time because of pushback.  And this is ignoring kicks and crossups canceling the spell completely).

Please reconsider the following: the absurd mana cost on Devouring Plague (relieving the need for inner focus), toning down stealth so that holy nova isn't mandatory, not moving all the mandatory pvp talents in shadow to be competing with each other, and lastly and most importantly - do not remove lightwell's ability to heal oneself, as that is the only thing keeping my priest able to solo farm.  Once every 15 minutes isn't OP enough to warrant such a nerf in my opinion.

As it stands, if the proposed priest changes make it live, I will likely uninstall... which would be incredibly disappointing as this server has a lot of good stuff going for it.

TLDR - Please reconsider the proposed priest changes from perspective of a hybrid spec pvp priest.

## Post 170014 by Gerganon — 2026-03-04T02:10:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=170014#p170014 | page 13 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

> **Kairi wrote: Mon Mar 02, 2026 1:31 am**
> Please don't move the blackout talent for priests deeper into the shadowtree. It's what makes hybrid priest viable!
>  Especially on troll it will be a big nerf. While already unfavourable.

Exactly my point from 3 comments up.  There will be 4 MANDATORY picks in shadow if blackout moves down, and there are already mandatory picks in other trees for pvp, like holy nova and inner focus (because of Devouring Plague costing far too much).

If blackout gets moved down, my 18/11/22 build will cease to exist.  It is a very fun and effective playstyle.  It is far from OP, so idk why it is getting nerfed like this.  (Changing lightwell is the biggest nerf to priest pvp and solo-play priests in general though).  If the proposed changes go live I assume that all non shadow priests will switch to full shadow for pvp, and all non-full shadow priests will only pve, because all specs need blackout, mindflay, silence, and mana burn to function ☆optimally☆ in pvp. But now you won't be able to get everything unless you are full shadow.

I hate full shadow because having 2 spell schools is what allows us to outplay these braindead melee classes.  They kick heal? Nows our chance to mind control, or make them pop a cd to stop it.  They kick Mc? Now I can heal my team for free (ignore the ridiculous casting pushback changes from vanilla).

I know I'm sounding condescending to the devs, I don't mean to.  But after so many years of vanilla, some of the tw changes make no sense.  I really love what you've done here, obviously I wouldn't be commenting I'd I didn't care about my character and the game.

I don't want to be full shadow.  And I only want to solo play pvp.  As far as I can see, if these changes go live, people like me are basically forced to roll a new character, or live with a completely unoptimal one.

## Post 170396 by Smokeybbq — 2026-03-09T13:17:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=170396#p170396 | page 13 | era: 1.18.1-announced-pre-release -->

With the upcoming itemization changes, please also take another look at the set bonuses for certain classes.

Paladin healing sets generally get strong and broadly useful bonuses across the board, while Druids, Priests, and Shamans often get bonuses tied to a specific spell that you rarely use (or force a playstyle or just with no impact)

In my opinion, Druid healing set should not be centered around Healing Touch, and Shaman healing should not be centered around Healing Wave. These bonuses should reflect how the classes are actually played.

Set bonuses should be something you are excited to get. They should feel rewarding and relevant.

P.S. Please also consider giving Shamans a weapon enchant for casters.

Love you all.

For a player like me who has played this game for 20 years, questing in the same zones gives me fatigue and there are some zones I would rather skip if possible. The xp nerf does not help with that.
> >
> >  Nerfing xp also means you have to quest way more
>
>   Right right because every class is able to aoe farm. Besides theres a reason why they tried to nerf some aoe farming patterns, cause is not the intention of turtle to solely rely on this xp.
>  A player like you hasnt played this game for 20 years cause turtle is not the same as vanilla wow so dont bring dumb statements
>  They already made clear that turtle wow is about the journey and right now getting to 60 in nord and amber is really fast if you already have 10 max level chars maybe its time to take a break or try a different wow version (tb, ascension, etc) or a diff game

So play slow and steady challenge and stop crying
[/quote]

I completely agree with this.
Turtle is a Vanilla+ and even though I've only been playing for a short time, I immediately understood the potential of this game. Creating a journey up to level 60 also means stopping looking at the levels as they go up.
I say this because the first character I made was normal. I immediately noticed that the level progression was very fast, so I struggled to complete certain dungeons. I imagine that in the summer things will be even worse.
All this is caused by these bloody “tents” that basically turn the game into a permanent x2 retail. I even found myself under a tent against my will. It was a nice trip but not very deep... I took it to 54 and got tired of it.
So now I've created three characters, all with hardcore + exhaustion + steady active. I've been playing for a month and hands down, I'm having the best experience ever. Every gaming session is always giving me opportunities! Whether it's 1 hour or 3 hours of play. Time finally doesn't matter anymore, I play because I enjoy it, regardless of the level I'm at. So I hope Turtle will make a firm decision about the exp boost.
I would also like to add that the halving of experience only applies to enemies. Missions remain with the same experience.
Translated with DeepL.com

Open Window Security in your right bottom taskbar, go to Virus and Scan protection > Allowed Threats > Protected Threats and select the files and click the Restore option

```
Move the game out of read-only folders such as Program Files, User Folder, Downloads, Desktop, etc.

Try to run WoW as Administrator!

If everything is done right, your Data folder should have Data\patch-9.mpq installed an your binary file should have revision 1.18.0

If it still doesn't work please use a direct download from our website.
Update is complete, starting the game...
```

## Post 172021 by Auralys — 2026-03-23T21:25:00Z
<!-- url: https://forum.turtlecraft.gg/viewtopic.php?p=172021#p172021 | page 14 | era: post-1.18.1 -->

Hello,
I’m posting this as a K40-geared Balance Druid (with Scythe of Elune), and even after the recent changes to Starfire crit chances, the spec still feels really off to play.

The main issue is that the gameplay just isn’t rewarding when you play it correctly !

Right now, when an Eclipse procs, you’re often forced to stopcast the spell you just started because letting it go through is a DPS loss. Since Eclipse windows are so short, you basically have to cancel your cast and immediately switch to the buffed spell.

At the same time, you’re expected to maintain your DoTs on the target, but you have no control over Eclipse procs, which makes some 12-second windows feel almost wasted.

That means optimal gameplay = constantly interrupting yourself.
It feels clunky, unintuitive, and frustrating.

On top of that, the spec feels way too RNG-dependent. More crit just means more procs, but also more situations where you have to react instantly or lose value. You don’t really gain control over your damage, it just becomes more chaotic. Threat generation also becomes very inconsistent because of this.

At the end of the day, you’re putting in more effort than most casters for equal or lower results. And unlike others, we don’t even have a real burst cooldown to stabilize our damage or create a strong window.

Another big issue is how inconsistent procs are with Balance spells.

Moonfire and Insect Swarm ticks don’t proc trinkets (Scythe, Clackora, Sigil), and Hurricane doesn’t either. Meanwhile, other classes can proc everything very easily with instant or low-commitment abilities (Arcane Explosion or Holy Nova, for example).

This makes DoTs feel underwhelming and AoE even worse, especially considering Hurricane is already harder to use properly (channeled, positioning-dependent, and punishing if mobs move).

I recently got the Clackora trinket, and honestly it feels awful on Balance, even though it should be a strong item. Even trying to stack it with rank 1 Wrath feels extremely inconsistent and unreliable.

I don’t think the spec needs more crit. It needs more consistency.

A few changes that would already improve the feel a lot:

Let Eclipse apply to the current cast instead of forcing stopcasting
(for example: the next Wrath after an Eclipse proc benefits from Arcane Eclipse, and the next Starfire benefits from Nature Eclipse — this would feel much more fluid and rewarding)
Allow DoTs (Moonfire / Insect Swarm) and Hurricane to proc trinkets and effects
Add a meaningful cooldown to give the spec some control over its damage
(for example: a cooldown that extends the current Eclipse by ~12 seconds every 3 minutes)

Right now, the spec feels clunky, RNG-heavy, and less rewarding than simpler alternatives.

Curious to hear if other Balance players feel the same, especially with raid gear and logs to compare.

Thanks for reading.
